## Document 013: 115年10月賦稅收入初步統計 (2026-11-11)

Report source: MOF
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

網際網路 https://www.mof.gov.tw同步發送 
下次發布日期：115 年 12 月 11 日下午 4 時

聯 絡 人： 陳 巧 芸 科 長 
電   話：02-2322-7555 
115年 10月賦稅收入初步統計 
一、整體賦稅收入 
（一）實徵淨額 
10 月實徵淨額 5,669 億元，較上年同月增加 1,495 億元；累計 1 至
10 月實徵 4 兆 1,383 億元，較上年同期增加 8,499 億元(+25.8%)，
主要由證交稅、營所稅及營業稅所貢獻。隨新版財政收支劃分法實
施，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率由 61.2%降為 4.5%(獲配稅額隨之減
少 2,662 億元)，致中央政府稅收增加數 3,906 億元(+15.8%)，不及
地方政府增加數 4,671 億元(+68.5%)。 
（二）預算達成率 
累 計1 至 10 月 實 徵 淨 額 占 全 年 預 算 數91.4% ， 其 中中 央 政 府為
92.8%，地方政府為 87.7%；與全年預算數差距分別為 3,913億元、
2,227億元、1,614億元。 
圖 1 賦稅實徵淨額及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）10 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營利事業所得稅增加 1,133 億元、
證券交易稅增加 220 億元及綜合所得稅增加 120 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,297
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 2,986 億元及營業稅增加 1,272 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-10 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)10 月實徵淨額 4,144 億元，較上年同月增加 1,133 億元(+37.6%)，
主因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 1 兆 4,172 億元，較上年同期增加 2,986
億元(+26.7%)，主因結算申報自繳 、暫繳稅款俱增及延分期稅款
陸續入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
10 月實徵淨額 614 億元，較上年同月增加 120 億元(+24.3%)，主因
扣繳稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 8,894 億元，較上年同
期增加 910 億元(+11.4%)，主因結算申報自繳稅額增加，以及盈餘分

配、薪資所得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
(1)10月受進口代徵稅額增加及適用 外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅增加
之交互影響，營業稅實徵淨額為負77億元，較上年同月(負146億元)
增加69億元。
(2)累 計1 至10 月 實 徵 淨額6,153 億 元， 較 上年 同 期 增 加1,272 億元
(+26.1%)，主因內需穩定 ，資通與視聽產品 、電子零組件等進口
代徵稅額增加，惟外銷營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

4.貨物稅
10月實徵淨額73億元，較上年同月減少41億元(-35.6%)，累計1至10
月實徵淨額948億元，較上年同期減少248億元(-20.7%)，主因新購汽
機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增加，加以汽柴油減徵幅度擴大所致。
5.證券交易稅
(1)10 月及累計 1 至 10 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 1,210
億元、1 兆 1,437 億元，分別較上年同期(6,267 億元、4,632 億元)
增 78.9%、1.5 倍。
(2)10 月證交稅實徵淨額 523 億元 ， 較上年同月增加 220 億元
(+72.6%)；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 5,583 億元，較上年同期增加
3,297 億元(+1.4 倍)。
6.關稅
10 月實徵淨額 132 億元，較上年同月增加 7 億元(+6.0%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 1,356 億元，較上年同期增加 88 億元(+6.9%)，均因機
械及電機設備、基本金屬、塑橡膠及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
10 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月減少 11 億元(-31.9%)，主因大額

稅款案件減少；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 234 億元，較上年同期增加
32 億元(+15.9%)，主因大額稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
10 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+5.9%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 560 億元，較上年同期減少
11 億元(-2.0%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.使用牌照稅
10 月實徵淨額 6 億元，較上年同月減少 1 億元(-10.2%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 676 億元，較上年同期減少 1 億元(-0.1%)，主因開徵
輛數減少，以及稅款入帳時間差異所致。
（三）前 10月主要稅目實徵淨額占全年預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%(已提前達成全年預算目標 )者有遺產稅 (123%)、贈與稅
(107%)、印花稅(106%)、房屋稅(105%)及營所稅(102%)。
2.低於 100%者有 牌照 稅(99%)、營業 稅(94%)、綜所 稅(93%)、契稅
(90%)、關稅(88%)、菸酒稅(81%)、證交稅(80%)、貨物稅(79%)、土

增稅(71%)及特銷稅(47%)等。

### Page 4

4
三、政府別賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 10 月中央政府賦稅收入 (不含中央統籌分配稅款 )實徵淨
額 2 兆 8,579 億元(占全年預算數 92.8%)，較上年同期增加 3,906 億元
(+15.8%)，主因新版財政收支劃分法 施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府 比率
大幅調降，獲配稅額隨之減少 2,662 億元，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加
3,297 億元、3,306 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 10 月地方政府賦稅
收入(含中央統籌分配稅款 )實
徵淨額 1 兆 1,487 億元(占全年
預算數 87.7%)，較上年同期增
加 4,671 億元(+68.5%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配 之營業稅
增加 4,013 億元所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 10 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 220 億元，較
上年同期增加 4 億元(+1.7%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 144
億元，較上年同期減少 6 億元(-4.0%)；撥入長照基金 953 億元(其中 575

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、220 億元來自菸稅，158 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.2%)。

公司簡介 
中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅 ， 早期業
務專注於傳統砂輪 ， 後取得鑽石碟技術專利
授權，並因應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，
切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，
砂輪事業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。
3Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻
記、KTC、MKS、MGT) 23%、DBU 32%、
SBU 45%。

主要客戶：台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導
體、英特爾，以及載板廠欣興等 
主要競爭對手：

王彥鈞 stanley.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

中砂 (1560 TT) 
以砂為鏡，可以升良率

永豐觀點 
以銅為鏡，可以正衣冠；以砂為鏡，可以升良率。 
 投資評價與建議 
目標價調升至 1,000 元：(1) 3Q26 ABU 獲利改善優於預期，帶動公司平均毛利率
升至 42.2%(+2.2pts QoQ) 且具延續力，(2) 產能建置加速，推動 2027 年獲利提
升，(3) 購置三峽廠，打開 DBU 擴產瓶頸。目標價隨 2028 年 EPS 調整，由 860
元(33x 2028 EPSF)上修至 1,000 元(32x 2028 EPSF)。

ESG 評析 
中砂企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅：中砂成立於 1953 年，2005 年上市，目前資
本額約 14.6 億元，早期業務專注於傳統砂輪，後取得鑽石碟技術專利授權，並因
應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，砂輪事
業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。3Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻記、
KTC、MKS、MGT) 23%、DBU 32%、SBU 45%。中砂總部位於新北市鶯歌區，
砂輪、鑽石相關產品主要在鶯歌、樹林、湖口廠生產，而再生 /測試晶圓主要在新
竹竹北、竹南廠生產，另在日本有 1 座廠房、泰國有 2 座廠房，另已在三峽新採
購 1 座廠房。客戶涵蓋半導體/記憶體製造商如台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導體、
英特爾，以及載板廠欣興等。 
3Q26 毛利率優於預期 ：中砂第三季稅後 EPS 3.91 元(+12.8%QoQ，+79.8%YoY)，
優於我們預期的 3.78 元，主因毛利率 42.2%季增約 2.2 個百分點高於預期，係來
自(1) 鑽石事業部營收占比及自身毛利率改善，係受到客戶惠先進製程出貨比重增
加，帶動高接產品放大，(2) ABU 產品組合改善，來自鑽石砂輪營收占比由40%提
升至約 50%。 
加速擴產並改善獲利：我們認為 4Q26 的毛利率仍有提升空間，係考量(1) 先進製
程及 HBM 對鑽石碟產品用量增加，且公司產能由3Q26 6.5 萬顆/月提升至 4Q26 
8 萬顆/月(原規劃 7 萬顆/月)，2nm/A14 用鑽石碟出貨量的增加將帶動 DBU 佔比
提升及獲利改善 ，(2) 部分測試晶圓及再生晶圓產品自 4Q26 起對所有客戶漲價約
10%， 加上測試晶圓比重回落 ， 可改善獲利率 ，(3) ABU 7 月漲價的效果可在 4Q26
顯現，加上鑽石砂輪、晶圓倒角砂輪、鑽石鍍膜等高階工具比重提升。另外，研究
部認為 2027 年中砂獲利成長動能仍將來自 DBU 的成長，目前公司規劃將鑽石碟
產能提升到明年底的 11 萬顆/月(+37.5%YoY)，以因應記憶體和美系 IDM 廠的擴
大採用、成熟製程客戶的產能擴增、以及晶圓代工廠先進製程積極擴產，同時預期
公司將適時與客戶們共同分擔生產成本的增加；SBU 則透過改造部分產線，並增
加表面缺陷檢測設備，來滿足客戶 HBM 對於特殊晶圓的需求。 
下一站，半導體用砂輪：由於既有樹林廠難以滿足客戶對鑽石碟中期需求，中砂於
今日董事會通過投入不超過 5.4 億新台幣於三峽購置廠房 ， 用於擴增鑽石碟及半導
體廠用的鑽石砂輪產能，因應 CoWoS/SoIC/HBM 等先進封裝和晶背供電應用，
我們認為有望於 2028 年起貢獻營收並改善 DBU 產品組合，並隨 HPC 產品(及搭
配的記憶體)封裝規格提升和製程轉進而成長。 
再次上修預估 ：研究部預估中砂 2026 年全年營收 103.2 億(+26.6%YoY)，毛利率
40.4%，稅後淨利 21.9 億(+61.0%YoY)，EPS 14.60 元(原估 14.47 元)，獲利上調
主因 ABU 獲利率改善速度優於預期。2027 年營收 133.6 億(+29.5%YoY)，毛利
率 45.4%，稅後淨利 33.2 億(+51.5%YoY)，EPS 22.08 元(原估 20.26 元)。2028
年營收 174.2 億(+30.4%YoY)，毛利率 47.8%，稅後淨利 47.6 億(+43.3%YoY)，
EPS 31.65 元(原估 26.05 元)。

營運現況與分析

## Document 050: 20261109 兆豐海外產業訊息評論-生成式AI模型開發商Anthropic(ANT US)上市前瞻

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261109 兆豐海外產業訊息評論-生成式AI模型開發商Anthropic(ANT US)上市前瞻.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 1 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
全球生成式 AI 模型開發商 Anthropic(ANT US)上市前瞻 
企業需求帶動營收高成長，算力成本考驗高估值

報告日期：2026.11.9 研究員：吳俞潔

10 月 26 日 Anthropic(ANT US) 向美國 SEC 提交 IPO 申請，11 月 2 日更
新第三季財報，市場預期公司將於 11 月底前上市，估值達 1.5 至 2 兆。
Anthropic 旗下 Claude 主要應用於企業 AI、 軟體開發及Agent 工作流程 ，
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求快速成長，26Q3 營收達 169.6 億(季增
47%)，2026 年前三季累計營收 332 億。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年營收有
望達 580 億 ， 若以Palantir 做為高成長的企業 AI 軟體作為基準，Anthropic
也受惠企業 AI 支出增加， 營收增速高於Palantir，但未來大量算力成本以及
現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司， 對應27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，Anthropic 
IPO 估值達 1.6 至 1.8 兆，引發讓市場對企業 AI 商業化及模型公司估值的想
像空間。 
不過，兆豐認為 Anthropic 雖然已經證明企業願意為 AI 模型付費，但目前
1.6 至 1.8 兆估值已經提前反應未來數年營收維持高速成長，以及算力成本
持續改善的預期。公司 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但因算力及基礎設施費用季增
77%，導致營益率由上季 19%降至 15%，顯示收入增加並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長。另外，公司已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾，未來仍然需要擴
大 Token 需求才能支撐相關支出，考量企業 AI 需求持續成長，但模型價格
競爭及算力成本有壓縮利潤率風險， 因此兆豐目前對Anthropic 的 IPO 估值
持中性看待。

Claude Code 帶動企業 AI 需求，Anthropic 收入主要來自 Token 用量 
Anthropic 成立於 2021 年，由前 OpenAI 研究人員 Dario Amodei 創立，主
要開發 Claude 系列 AI 模型，透過 Claude 訂閱服務、企業 API 及 Claude 
Code 產品取得收入。相比傳統 SaaS 以固定訂閱費用為主，Anthropic 80%
營收來自 Token 使用量計價 ， 訂閱收入占20%， 企業可以透過AWS、Google 
Cloud 的雲端平台採購 Claude 服務，CSP 客戶占公司收入 48%，這種模式
使得 Anthropic 能隨著企業 AI 使用量增加而擴大收入，但也導致營收受到
Token 價格及企業使用量變動影響。 
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求成長，過去 12 個月支出超過 10 萬的客
戶達 11,900 家，其中超過 1,900 家年支出超過 100 萬，250 家超過 1,000

### Page 2

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 2 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
萬，企業 AI 支出正轉向更大規模的付費使用，也帶動 Anthropic 營收快速成
長。

估值達 1.8 兆，Token 價格決定高成長能否持續 
Anthropic 2026 年前三季營收達 332 億(年增 12 倍)，26Q3 營收 170 億(季
增 47%)，但相比 Q2 季增 144%成長大幅放緩。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年
營收達 580 億，若以 Palantir 作為高成長企業 AI 軟體的估值參考 ，合理倍數
落在 27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，對應 IPO 估值 1.6 至 1.8 兆，兆豐認為雖然
Anthropic 營收成長的確遠高於多數軟體公司，Claude Code 及企業 API 的
高需求也支持未來營收持續擴張，但但模型訓練及推論需要持續投入大量算
力 ， 獲利率及現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司，以 2026 年 580 億營收估算，
隱含 28 至 31 倍 Forward P/S，反應了市場不只預期公司維持高成長，也預
期未來利潤率能隨著規模擴大而改善。 
Anthropic 80%收入來自 Token 用量計價 ， 未來營收不只取決於企業使用量 ，
也包含模型能否維持目前的高階定價， 外資8 月調查 100 名歐美大型企業 AI
預算決策者，92%預期未來 12 個月 Token 預算增加，但若 Token 價格上漲
50%，84%企業會優化使用或轉換其他模型，若價格下降 50%，86%企業則
願意增加使用量，反應企業 AI 需求仍有成長空間，但客戶對價格相當敏感，
尤其開放權重模型及低成本模型持續改善，企業有機會將部分工作轉向更為
便宜的模型，即使整體 Token 使用量增加，但開放權重和低成本模型競爭，
Anthropic 面臨單價競爭導致營收增速放緩的風險，因此兆豐認為目前 1.6 至
1.8 兆估值已經反應 Anthropic 長期維持高成長及較高 Token 價格的預期，
但如果模型競爭加劇，導致價格下降速度超過增加的使用量，將有估值下修
風險。

接近 9,000 億的算力承諾，收入成長未必同步轉為獲利 
Anthropic 為了支撐 Claude 模型訓練及企業推論需求 ， 持續擴大與Amazon、
Google、Microsoft 及其他 AI 基礎設施供應商的合作 ， 並且透過Nvidia GPU、
Google TPU 及 AWS Trainium 分散算力來源，Anthropic 目前即時可用算力
達 2.7GW，與 OpenAI 的 3.2GW 差距縮小，近期再透過 Nscale、Lambda
及 SpaceX 取得 1.1GW 的新增算力協議。不過，算力擴張也帶來龐大的長
期採購支出 ，Anthropic 已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾 ， 其中4,475 億預
計在 2030 年底前兌現， 未來12 個月到期的承諾達 698 億，合約有最低採購
要求，即使未來需求不如預期，公司仍然需要負擔相關成本。 
從財務表現來看，Anthropic 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但算力及基礎設施費用季
增 77%，營益率也由 19%下降至 15%，顯示新增收入並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長，雖然公司提到部分成本增加來自短期高價算力採購，未來隨著長
期合約及專用資料中心陸續上線，單位算力成本有機會下降，但兆豐認為即
使採購成本改善，也不代表利潤率一定回升，主要因 Anthropic 提前承諾大
規模算力支出，但未來 Token 收入仍然取決於企業實際使用量及模型定價，

### Page 3

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 3 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
若新增算力無法帶來足夠的 Token 收入 ， 固定採購成本將壓縮獲利及現金流。 
因此兆豐認為 Anthropic 目前估值不只反應營收持續高成長，也隱含未來算
力成本下降能帶動利潤率改善，但考量 26Q3 營收增速大幅放緩，營益率下
滑、未來長期算力採購承諾龐大，以及 Token 需求成長無法抵銷模型降價及
新增算力成本 ， 將壓縮利潤率 ， 不足以支撐更高的高成長成熟企業 AI 軟體公
司估值，因此兆豐對 1.6 至 1.8 兆的 IPO 估值持中性看待

（本資料僅供內部參考，不作為投資決策依據）

## Document 088: 2026年7-9月期の実質GDP～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期の実質GDP～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

（前期比、％）
（年・四半期）
実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移
民間最終消費支出 民間企業設備 その他民需 公需 外需
（資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」
( )内は年率換算値
(注）2602までは当研究所による改定見込値、2603は予測値
予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として 輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。 
2026年 7-9月期の地域別輸出数量指数を季節調整
値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％） 、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％） 、アジア向けが前期
比▲0.3％（4-6 月期：同▲0.8％） 、うち中国向けが

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

GCE’s 3Q26 core business (OPI) was 6%/1% below GSe/BBG 
consensus due mainly to (1) key AI customer’s product transition 
period (revenue in 3Q was 4/3% lower than GSe/BBG consensus), 
and (2) the increasing investment in R&D to improve its 
production capability on AI HDI and N+M MLB products), despite 
the much stronger than expected GM (0.8/1.8ppt higher than 
GSe/BBG consensus). For the net income in 3Q26, it was 5% below 
our bullish estimate, resulting from the weaker than expected 
operating profit, but it was 3% higher than BBG consensus, thanks to 
the lower than expected tax rate resulting from the increasing profit 
contribution from the low tax rate Thailand plant (tax rate was only 
at 25% in 3Q26 vs. BBG consensus at ~30%).  
For 4Q26, we expect the company’s revenue to grow by 7% QoQ, 
due to ongoing key AI customers’ product transition, and the fewer 
working days due to the holiday season. We expect GCE’s GM will 
further expand to 39.2% (up 0.5ppt QoQ or 5.5ppt YoY) thanks to 
the better product mix (high-end networking demand should ramp 
up more significantly from 4Q26, per GSe), despite the increasing 
cost from high-end CCL (see here).  
Investment view - GCE will continue to deliver a better margin 
profile with expanding market share in AI ASIC market 
We continue to expect GCE will hold the major share for Trainium 3 
(70%+ from 3Q26), and see an even better market share in the next 
generation Trainium 4 products (as key competitors are all unable to 
support high-end AI HDI products) after the company ramps up its 
AI HDI production yield rate. We also expect the company to start 
supplying the new TPU v8 PCB products, which should meaningfully 
improve the company’s market share in coming quarters (from <5% 
in 2026 to 20-30%+ in 2027), suggesting an even better GM outlook 
in the foreseeable future.  
On the other hand, the company will only expand its capacity

volume by ~20% in 2027, without considering the product mix improvement/ASP hike 
(we believe after considering the better ASP for next-gen AI products, the company’s 
capacity value should go up by 35%+ YoY in 2027), while we believe the AI PCB 
volume/area demand should at least increase by 50% YoY, implying a potential market 
share loss in coming quarters. We note Greater China peers are also expanding capacity 
in SEA regions to try to penetrate into GCE’s key AI ASIC markets, implying increasing 
competition in coming quarters, which could lead to less favorable ASP in the long term, 
despite no significant impact for now. However, considering GCE’s best in class AI PCB 
production technology and its proactive investment in the high-end PCB R&D for more 
than 10 years, as well as the company’s first mover advantage in the SEA PCB market, we 
expect GCE will continue to have opportunities to expand its business in the most 
high-end PCB market, suggesting a good GM outlook in the long term.  
Maintain Buy on GCE, with a new 12-m TP of NT$1,660 (from NT$1,800).

Earning and TP revision

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026/27/28E net income estimate by 9%/6%/5% to factor in the 
potential slower pricing hike outlook in the PCB industry, considering the increasing 
competition in the AI PCB industry (cut 2026/27/28E revenue estimate by 7/6/5%). 
Moreover, we believe GCE will need to invest more in R&D projects in the coming 
quarters to improve its HDI production capability, and expect the N+M PCB R&D 
investment will also drive OPEX in coming years, considering the increasing adoption 
rate from 1H27 (revise up 2027 OPEX to sales ratio by 0.3ppt).

### Page 4

TP revision 
Maintain Buy with a new 12-m TP of NT$1,660 (from NT$1,800), based on 15x P/E 
(down from 20x previously, still in line with AI PCB peers’ average valuation); we also 
rollover our valuation base from 2027E to 2H27-1H28E, and factor in our earnings 
revision.

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

GCE is a key cloud (server+switch) PCB supplier globally with 20%+ market share in 
2022. We expect cloud PCB market demand to grow at a 15% 2024-26E CAGR (far 
stronger than the overall RPCB+HDI market demand growth rate at 5% 2024-26E 
CAGR). We believe GCE’s proactive capacity expansion plan (33% in 2026) and high 
annualized ROI (300%+) will continue to drive the company’s revenue/margin level in the 
coming quarters/years. Additionally, we believe GCE can continue to see strong demand 
from the increasing content per server/switch, as we expect both markets to see a 
20-50%+ layer count increase after the generation upgrade (upgrade to 400G/Eagle 
Stream). We are constructive on GCE’s long-term growth opportunity given its solid 
position in the rapidly growing cloud PCB market, and we are Buy rated on the stock. 
GCE is trading higher than its historical P/E average level driven by strong AI server 
demand, but lower than the TW AI server players’ average. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,660 is based on a 15x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with AI PCB peers average valuation).  
Key downside risks: (1) weaker-than-expected AI server market demand; (2) delays in 
the company’s Nvidia OAM/UBB product qualification, (3) weaker-than-expected HDI 
demand, and (4) higher-than-expected ramp up cost for new capacity

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components

Solid 3Q26 results; positive tone to 4Q26/ 
2027; reiterate Buy; trim PO to NT$3,650

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,650 TWD | Price: 2,790 TWD

3Q26: record-high GM/OPM for five straight quarters 
Asia Vital Components (AVC)’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 33.9%/28.5% hit a record high for the 
fifth straight quarter, exceeding BofAe of 33.0%/27.5% and consensus of 33.3%/27.9%. 
OP (+25% QoQ, +108% YoY) was 7%/4% above BofAe/consensus, with higher sales 
(+20% QoQ, +52% YoY; 3%/2% above BofAe/consensus). We attribute the results beat 
to solid product mix (server and networking comprised 66% of 3Q26 sales, same as 1Q-
2Q26) and improving production efficiency. Despite a non-operating loss (mainly FX 
loss), NP was 10%/3% above consensus (Exhibit 3).  
Positive tone to 4Q26/2027; ongoing capacity expansion 
We expect 4Q26 sales to rise 19% QoQ and 2027 sales to grow 40% YoY, with mgmt.’s 
still positive tone to 4Q26/2027, mainly driven by AI liquid cooling projects. AVC 
indicated full support to clients’ platform upgrade and shipment pipeline. It should be 
able to pass through the higher raw material cost. In 2027, mgmt. targets ASIC sales mix 
to surpass GPU. AVC also sees solid sales momentum from chassis/rack and CDU, acting 
as the solution provider. Mgmt. maintained 2026 capex guidance at NT$15bn and guided 
NT$15-20bn for 2027 (BofAe unchanged at NT$17bn), mainly for capacity expansion in 
Vietnam. Mgmt. now targets another 50% cold plate capacity expansion in 2027. 
Trim PO to NT$3,650 for a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 3-10%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 results beat and higher 
margins, driven by ongoing server/networking project ramp-up. Yet, we cut our PO to 
NT$3,650 (23x 2027E EPS), with a lower P/E multiple (2SD above average since 2020; 
Exhibit 5) from NT$3,888 (was 27x; 3SD above) on AI supply chain’s potentially higher 
shipment/pipeline uncertainty due to component shortage. Nevertheless, we reiterate 
Buy on AVC as we believe it should remain one of the key beneficiaries from rising 
liquid-cooling adoption and ongoing GPU/ASIC platform/spec upgrade. We also highlight 
solid sales/margin growth under broad product base and diversified project/client base.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
AVC is the leading system-level thermal/cooling component 
maker globally. Key end markets include server, PC, 
smartphone. Key end clients include global CSPs, PC brands, 
smartphone brands. AVC was founded in 1991 and went 
publicly listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange on 27 Sep 2002. It 
is headquartered in Taiwan. Its main production sites are 
located in China (Shenzhen, Wuhan, Shanghai, Dongguan, 
Chengdu), with ongoing capacity expansion in Vietnam.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to AVC given its leading position in 
thermal/cooling component and total solution strategy to 
ride on strong AI market demand, double-digit sales/EPS 
CAGR in 2026-28 with rising liquid cooling sales mix and 
ASP/margin increase.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Asia Vital Components (AVMPF) 
Our PO of NT$3,650 is based on 23x 2027E EPS. We use 23x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since 2020) in view of AVC's double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. We 
believe AVC's leading position in thermal/cooling component market and strong AI 
market demand will drive rising liquid cooling sales mix and ASP/margin increase.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) stronger demand in AI server, server, PC, smartphone, (2) 
better operating leverage with larger revenue scale, (3) less intensive competition, (4) 
faster product certification, (5) faster capacity expansion, (6) favorable FX movement 
(USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to help on GM and/or non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/component 
shortage, (2) weaker server/PC/smartphone market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) GM pressure due to higher material/labor/utility cost, (4) more 
intensive competition, (5) delayed product certification, (6) slower capacity expansion, 
(7) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-
operating items.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things , 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst ’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating) 
Ratings dispersion guidelines for coverage clusterR2 
Buy ≥ 10% ≤ 70% 
Neutral ≥ 0% ≤ 30% 
Underperform N/A ≥ 20% 
R2Ratings dispersions may vary from time to time where BofA Global Research believes it better reflects the investment prospects of stocks in a Coverage Cluster. 
INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

Hiwin Technologies Corp.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Beat on Margin; Up-cycle
Continues to Play Out
Signs are pointing to ongoing revenue growth and decent GM
heading into 2027.  Stay OW.
3Q26 earnings beat thanks to better GM: See Results Summary .
Momentum being sustained into 2027: Management guides for 4Q26 sales to
grow QoQ (vs. seasonality of down QoQ) and 2027 to be better than 2026,
supported by strong order momentum and slightly lengthening delivery lead time of

6.5 months for ball screws and 4-6 months for linear guides. That said, Hiwin is not
able to fulfill all of the orders on hand because of the labor supply constraints.
Meanwhile, It holds a positive view on the margin outlook because the revenue mix
should be optimized  amid solid demand, though it has no plan to raise prices again
in the near term.
More sub-system level shipments: Besides the traditional linear guides and ball
screws, Hiwin is increasing its sub-systems offerings integrated with motors, sensors,

etc., for  precise positioning systems in CPO alignment and multiple axis rotary
tables in military, satellite, and material processing applications. These solutions
have gained more and more traction among Japanese and Chinese machine tool
customers
Robotics business one of the key drivers: The robotics segment has been Hiwin's
fastest-growing segment in the past few years and will remain  one of the major
drivers. It has risen from 7% of the revenue mix in 2024 to 10% in 2025 and is on

track for 12% in 2026 and higher in future. First, wafer transport systems are riding
on the strong demand from semiconductor fabs. For humanoid robots, it can supply
various components including ball screws, linear guides, reducers, motors, drivers,
etc.; it has received small volume orders from one major US  customer. Last, the
logistics robot project, originally scheduled for late 2026, will be pushed out
because of spec change.
Stay OW: We remain constructive on Hiwin stock. Near-term sentiment should

Results Summary
Off revenue of NT$8,110mn (+8% QoQ/+35% YoY), GM expanded 2.2ppt QoQ, to 36.3%,
surpassing both our estimate and consensus at 35.2%, driven by the tariff reimbursement
(0.6ppt boost), higher utilization, price hike tailwinds, and more favorable FX (0.5ppt
help).
Opex was well controlled at NT$1,502mn (opex ratio 18.5%), leading to  operating profit of
NT$1,446mn (OPM 17.8%), 19% above our estimate at NT$1,210mn (OPM 15.1%) and 18%

above consensus at NT$1,227mn (OPM 15.2%).
With non-op gains of NT$134mn, NP reached NT$1,230mn (EPS NT$3.48), 23% above both
our estimate and consensus at NT$998mn (EPS NT$2.82) and NT$1,001mn (EPS NT
$2.83), respectively.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.

n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

ab 11 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Nan Ya Plastics
Glass fabric capacity expansion to strengthen
vertical integration
What's new
NYP announced plans to invest NT$11.5bn to add 102mn meters of glass fabric capacity
(+17% vs. existing 590mn meters capacity) in Zhangbin, Taiwan, by end-2029.
According to management, the expansion aims to meet growing demand from AI
servers, networking equipment and increasingly complex PCBs, with 60–70% of the

additional capacity allocated for internal use. Capacity will flexibly serve conventional E-
glass and AI-grade specialty glass fabric, with specialty products targeted to reach ~40%
of glass fabric revenue in 2-3 years. NYP is also expanding upstream yarn production and
adding over 500 weaving looms to support its planned glass fabric capacity expansion
by end-2029.
Implications
NYP: Glass fabric expansion to strengthen vertical integration; 1.5–2.0% EM OP

upside from 2030
We view the expansion positively, as glass fabric remains the most supply-constrained
product across NYP's CCL value chain, with the highest OPM of c40% in 3Q26 for glass
fabric. We expect further margin improvement in 4Q26, driven by continued price hikes
and a higher specialty glass fabric mix amid strong AI-related demand. Over the longer
term, the expansion should strengthen NYP's vertical integration and secure upstream
glass fabric supply for downstream CCL production. Assuming full utilization and

current ASP and margins, we estimate the 17% capacity expansion could lift EM OP by
1.5–2.0% from 2030 onwards, with further upside potential from specialty product mix
upgrades.
E-glass fabric: limited impact on external supply
NYP's planned capacity expansion of 102mn meters per year is equivalent to ~2% of
total industry capacity according to our estimates. We expect limited impact on external
E-glass supply, as NYP plans to use 60-70% of output internally, leaving only ~30-40mn

meters annually for external sales before accounting for specialty fabric allocation.
AI-grade specialty fabric: supply constraints likely to persist
We expect supply tightness to persist amid specialty yarn constraints and qualified fabric
production ramp-up. NYP's undisclosed capacity mix further limits visibility on
incremental AI-grade fabric supply.
Valuation: Maintain Buy and PT at NT$390/share
NYP is trading at 10x 27E P/E and 3.5x 27E P/B on our estimates.  Our 26/27E dividend

yields are 3.8%/6.3% at the current share price. We expect limited near-term valuation
impact from the glass fabric expansion, given its expected completion by end-2029.

Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya Plastics one of the core
companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals,
plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad laminates (CCL)

producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned
subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya
Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns
23% of Formosa Petrochemical.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nan Ya Plastics:
We base our price target for NYP on a SOTP valuation.
Key upside risks include: 1) stronger CCL pricing; 2) faster high-end CCL mix upgrades; and 3)

a faster petchem margin recovery.
Key downside risks are: 1) a CCL pricing reversal on faster supply additions or weaker
demand; 2) slower AI qualification/ramp of the company's CCL business; and 3) further
weakening chemicals/polyester businesses.
Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board:
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-

expected pricing increase, better-than-estimated consumer demand, and continued tightness
in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial

intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the
industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
Nanya Technology:
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over the next 12
months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA)  is the one-year local
market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR) Stocks may be flagged
by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

performance record and discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record and discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

26.3Q Review: 수익성에 대한 입증이 필요  Company Brief

26.3Q Review: 매출액 31.6조원, 영업이익 1.8조원  
기아는 26.3Q 매출액 31.6조원 (+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원 
(+24.3% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, +5.8% OPM) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 26.3Q 영업이익은 
시장 컨센서스 (2.36조원)를 22.9% 하회했다 [iM증권 추정치: 매출액 31.5조원, 
영업이익 2.3조원]. 주요 구성 요소별 내용은 아래와 같다. 
① P: 26.3Q 26.3Q 전동화 차량 판매는 30.0만대 (+47% YoY, 판매 비중 
36.2%)를 기록했는데, 이 중 HEV 17.2 만대 (+46% YoY), EV 12.3 만대 
(+75% YoY)로 구성됨. 미국 HEV 판매가 전년 동기 대비 118% 증가하며 
시장 성장률을 약 4배 상회했고, 특히 서유럽 EV 판매가 전년 대비 163% 
증가함. 단 26.3Q 중 원화가 빠르게 강세로 전환되며 원/달러, 원/유로 
평균 환율은 전분기 대비 각 5.2%, 6.2% 하락함에 따른 – 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매는 84.7만대 (+7.9% YoY) 를 기록했고, 북미, 유럽, 
인도 등 주요 시장에서 고른 성장 나타남. 26.3Q 소매 판매 또한 82.9만대 
(+6.9% YoY)로 글로벌 산업 수요 (-6% YoY)를 상회. 북미는 HEV, 내수와 
유럽은 EV, 인도는 신형 셀토스와 소넷을 중심으로 판매가 확대됨 (이하 
26.3Q 권역별 도매 판매 YoY, 국내: +2.2%, 북미: +7.6%. 유럽: +19.3%. 
인도: +38.5%). 이란 사태 장기화로 아중동 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 
28.4% 감소했으나, 수요가 견조한 중남미와 CIS로 물량을 전환하며 
감소분을 상당 부분 만회함. 연간 도매 판매 가이던스 (335만대)를 유지함. 
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 약 0.7조원으로 추정되며, 전년 동기 대비 
5,000억원 감소함 (25.2Q 0.7조원, 25.3Q 1.2조원, 25.4Q 1.0조원, 26.1Q 
0.8조원). 반면 인센티브 증가 및 가격 인하 7,270억원, 서유럽 EV 비중 
확대에 따른 믹스 악화 4,650억원, 원자재 가격 상승 영향 3,680억원 등 
총 1.6조원의 감익 요인이 발생함. 이에 26.3Q 매출원가율은 84.1%로 
전년 동기 대비 3.0%p 상승함 (인센티브 +1.9%p, 재료비 +1.2%p). 다만 
26.3Q 기말 환율 급락하며 판매보증충당부채 환입 규모가 축소되었고 
판매보증비 약 9,000억원 감소. 판매관리비율은 10.1%로 3.7%p 개선됨. 
26.3Q 기아 순이익은 1.29조원으로 전년 동기 대비 9.6% 감소했다. 원화 강세에 
따라 외환환산 관련 이익이 전년 동기 540억원 이익에서 3,718억원으로 손실 
전환됐고, 현대모비스 등 관계사 이익 감소로 지분법손익 또한 360억원 감소했기 
때문이다 (현대차 순이익 감소 논리와 동일). 1) 산업 수요 감소와 중동 판매 차질에도 
권역별 파워트레인 대응을 통해 그룹사 대비 가지고 있는 외형 성장성을 증명했으나, 
2) 유럽 EV 판매 확대 과정에서 발생한 인센티브와 믹스 악화라는 비용 부담, 원자재 
가격 상승이 관세 감소 효과를 대부분 반납했고, 3) 분기 중 급격한 원화 강세가 
더해지며 시장 기대를 크게 하회하는 수익성을 기록했다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
Buy (Maintain)

실적 발표 후 주가 급락: 성장에도 불구하고 수익성에 대한 우려  
26.10.30 실적 발표 당일 기아 주가는 11.7% 하락했다. 하루 전 발표된 그룹사 실적 
발표와 상반된 모습이었다. 시장이 이번 실적에서 확인한 우려는 아래와 같다. 첫째, 
성장과 이익의 괴리다. 관세 영향을 제외한 26.3Q 영업이익은 2.52조원으로 전년 
동기(2.70조원) 대비 오히려 1,770억원 감소했다. 판매 증가에 따른 물량 효과 
(+4,420억원)와 환율 효과 (+5,290억원)가 발생했음에도, 인센티브와 믹스 악화로 이를 
대부분 반납한 것이다. 기아의 외형 성장 상당 부분이 유럽 EV 판매 증가에서 나오는데, 
해당 시장에서는 중국 EV 업체와의 경쟁으로 공격적인 프로모션이 불가피하다. 26.3Q 
유럽 권역에서의 중소형 EV 판매가 사업 계획을 크게 웃돌며 EV 믹스는 13.1%에서 
14.8%로 확대됐다. 시장은 이를 M/S를 위해 수익성을 훼손하고 있다고 받아들이는 
것이다. 둘째, 당장 26.4Q에도 유의미한 수익성 개선이 없을 것이라는 점이다. 기아는 
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 4Q 영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이며, 연초 
제시한 연간 가이던스 (영업이익 10.2조원, +8.3% OPM) 에 다소 미달할 것이라고 
밝혔다. 중동 분쟁의 영향이 최소 연말까지 이어지고, 원자재와 물류비 부담 또한 
지속될 것이 회사의 판단이다. 무엇보다 원/달러 환율이 26.2Q 평균 약 1,505원에서 
26.3Q 약 1,430원, 현재 1,320~1,340원 수준까지 하락하며 이에 따른 - 영향이 커질 
수 있다. 셋째, 그룹사와의 이익 체력 비교다. 현대차는 26.3Q 파업과 생산 차질로 도매 
판매가 11.3% 감소했음에도 원가 절감을 통해 영업이익이 5.5% 증가했고, 시장 
기대를 상회했다. 반면 기아는 판매가 7.9% 증가했음에도 영업이익이 컨센서스를 20% 
이상 하회했다. 26.3Q 양사 영업이익 격차는 약 8,600억원까지 벌어진 상황이다.  
단기 노이즈를 제외한다면 기아의 기업 가치는 여전히 높다  
그럼에도 당사는 기아의 EV 전략, 낮은 수익성을 이유로 중장기 기업 가치에 대한 
기대를 낮출 이유는 없다고 판단한다. 첫째, 외부 요인을 걷어내면 본원적인 수익 
창출력은 유지되고 있다. 기아는 26.3Q 영업이익률을 낮춘 외부 요인을 원자재 1.2%p, 
중동 판매 차질 0.7%p, 환율 1.7%p 등 총 3.6%p로 분석했다. 즉 중동 분쟁이 
해소되면 상당 부분이 사라질 일회성 요인이다. 둘째, EV 수익성 개선 경로는 HEV가 
걸어온 길과 같다. 기아는 HEV 역시 도입 초기에는 수익을 내지 못했으나 현재는 ICE에 
버금가는 수익성을 내고 있다며, 26~27 년 현재의 EV 전략을 유지하겠다고 밝혔다. 
유럽 ICE 시장은 26.3Q 전년 동기 대비 약 20% 축소된 반면 EV 시장은 50% 이상 
성장하고 있다. 전환기에 스케일을 확보하지 못하면 EV 마진을 개선할 방법도 없다. 
기아는 향후 3년간 배터리, 모터, 히트펌프 등 주요 부품에서 연 10% 수준의 원가 
절감을 추진하고, 2~3년 내 EV를 HEV와 같은 수익성 경로에 올려놓겠다는 계획이다. 
셋째, 중장기 성장 계획을 수정하지 않았다. 기아는 27년 사업 계획이 CID 당시 제시한 
숫자와 큰 차이가 없을 것이며, 중장기 영업이익률 10% 목표도 유지했다. IAA 시행에 
대비해 슬로바키아 공장에서 EV를 전량 생산하고 ICE 국내 수출 방안도 검토 중이다. 
주주환원 측면에서도 TSR 35% 를 지키고, 자사주 매입보다 배당을 우선하겠다는 
입장이다. 10/30 종가와 최소 DPS 기준만으로도 배당수익률은 6.3%에 달하고 있다.

기아 판매관리비 (좌) 판매관리비율 (우)
  26.4Q 기아 매출액 31.8조원, 영업이익 2.2조원 전망 
기아 26.4Q 실적을 매출액 31.8조원 (+13.2% YoY), 영업이익 2.2조원 (+20.8% YoY, 
+7.0% OPM)으로 전망한다. 26.4Q 판매 성장세는 이어지고, 3Q에 집중 반영된 원자재 
부담 일부 완화와 계절적 성수기 효과로 3Q 대비 수익성은 소폭 개선될 것이다. 다만 
1) 원화 강세에 따른 평균 환율 하락, 2) 유럽 EV 인센티브의 높은 수준 유지 (미국은 
할부 금리 상승으로 QoQ 약 200달러 증가, 유럽은 전분기와 유사), 3) 중동 판매 
차질과 원자재, 물류비 부담 지속을 감안하면 의미 있는 반등은 어렵다. 이는 4Q 
영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이라는 회사의 언급과도 궤를 같이한다. 
기아 목표주가 150,000원으로 하향  
기아 목표주가를 26.4Q 추정치 하향을 반영해 150,000원으로 하향한다. 기아의 판매 
경쟁력은 금번 분기에도 증명됐다. 다만 EV 판매 확대가 수익성 개선으로 연결된다는 
점이 숫자로 확인되기 전까지, 시장은 기아의 성장에 할인율을 적용할 것이다. 중동 
분쟁 해소와 EV 원가 절감이 맞물리는 27년이 기아가 수익성을 증명할 시점이 될 
것이며, 6%를 상회하는 배당수익률이 그때까지 주가의 하방을 지지할 것으로 판단된다.

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.

 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

주가 추이

3Q26 매 출액 과 영 업 이익 은 비현 금 성 비용 항목 개 선 으로 시장 기대치
를 상 회 했 으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인 플 레 이션 등으로 연간 가이 던 스 는 
하 향 되 었 습니 다. 올해 생산 차질 에 따라 이 연된 신차 판 매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시 화 하 는 부분 에 주목 필 요 합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 Y oY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제 외 하 면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년 으 로 이연된 것 이 라 는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본 격 화 된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로 컬 업 체 들 의 공세를 방 어 하 는 모 습 을 
확인하는 것이 가 능 할 것으로 판 단 한 다. 노 후 차 종 중심 운 영 에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근 원 적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

## Document 279: AI投资步入“幻灭谷底”，未来18个月将是生死大考！

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI投资步入“幻灭谷底”，未来18个月将是生死大考！.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛认为， AI 投资周期正处于 “ 幻灭⾕底 ” ，但这并⾮⾸次。核⼼⽭盾在于：新云计算商的商
业模式⾼度依赖终端客⼾ AI ⽀出回报，⽽⽬前 “ 能将盈利归因于 AI 的公司基本上是零 ” 。
Social Capital 创始⼈ Palihapitiya 明确划定 18 个⽉关键窗⼝，届时市场将完成对可持续与
不可持续 AI 商业模式的清算。
摘要：
每⼀轮计算基础设施的⼤规模投资，最终都会⾛到同⼀个路⼝ —— 市场开始质疑回报。
⾼盛 11 ⽉ 10 ⽇的研报指出，当前 AI 投资正处于 " 幻灭⾕底 " （ trough of disillusionment ）。权益市
场和信⽤市场的近期波动、⽇趋复杂的循环融资结构，以及美国中期选举后新的监管不确定
性，共同推动市场对 AI 资本⽀出能否持续产⽣疑问。
该⾏美国互联⽹股票研究⾸席分析师 Eric Sheridan 直接说：" 每⼀轮计算周期我分析下来都是同
⼀个弧线 —— 产品初期的兴奋、投资规模的扩张，然后是对回报的审视。 "

他强调，这不是这轮周期第⼀次出现幻灭情绪。过去 12 到 18 个⽉，围绕 AI 主题已出现过三次市
场回调：去年秋冬、今年夏天，以及当下。但他也坦承： " 赌注⽆疑更⾼了，因为我们在周期中
⾛得更远，投⼊的资⾦也已⼤幅增加。 "
Social Capital 创始⼈兼 CEO Chamath Palihapitiya 在报告中直⾔： " 我看到的是数据中⼼承诺容
量、实际容量与回报之间的严重错配。 "他警告，未来 18 个⽉将是⼀场 " 清算 "—— 市场将逐步区
分出哪些 AI 商业模式和基础设施承诺是可持续的，哪些是不可持续的。
新云计算商的致命脆弱性
Chamath Palihapitiya 对当前压⼒最集中的领域 —— 新云计算商（ Neoclouds ） —— 作出了详细
剖析。
" 我看到了数据中⼼承诺容量、实际容量与回报之间的错配， " 他说， " 这对新云计算商⽽⾔尤为
关键，它们在 GPU 、电⼒和数据中⼼容量上做出了巨⼤承诺，⽽⽀撑这些承诺所需的需求，最
终取决于终端客⼾能否从 AI ⽀出中获得有意义的回报。 "

Palihapitiya ⽤航空业打⽐⽅：GPU 是发动机，数据中⼼是机⾝，供电⼟地是机场，算⼒是按⼩
时出售的座位 —— ⽽⼀个卖不出去的算⼒⼩时，就像⼀个空座位，永远消失了。新云计算商的
盈亏平衡利⽤率约为 70-75% ，超出这⼀⽔平才能真正盈利。
他进⼀步指出，当前最⼤的问题在于需求侧：企业 AI 项⽬实施困难，中层员⼯抵触，⽣产率提升
⼜往往在价值链的其他环节流失。 " 今天，能将盈利或利润增⻓归因于 AI 的公司，基本上是零。 "
18 个⽉，清算窗⼝
Palihapitiya 对时间窗⼝的判断相当明确：" 未来 18 个⽉⾄关重要。我们不会在那时得到最终答
案，但我们会为⼀个清算过程奠定基础，将可持续的 AI 商业模式和基础设施承诺与不可持续的
区分开来。 "
他解释，这⼀窗⼝的关键在于：更多 AI 产业链上的主要企业将进⼊公开市场的审视视野，投资者
将能更清晰地判断这些公司的商业模式能否持续扩张。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⼀个值得追踪的指标是：主要模型提供商的收⼊结构，是继续以 " 按 token 消费 " 为主，还是
向 " 按结果定价 " 转型？ " 订阅制不等于结果定价，但⽅向上更接近， " 他说。
超⼤规模云计算商：仍能实现 15% 以上回报？
尽管压⼒重重，⾼盛对超⼤规模云计算商（ Hyperscalers ）的判断相对乐观。
Sheridan 表⽰： " 即便如此，我们估计六⼤美国超⼤规模云计算商可以轻松实现 15% 以上的投资
资本回报率（ ROIC ）。 " 这六家包括 Alphabet 、亚⻢逊、微软、 Meta 、甲⻣⽂和 SpaceX ，对应
2028 ⾄ 2030 年的累计营收约为 1.42 万亿美元。
截⾄ 2026 年三季度， AWS 、 Azure 和⾕歌云三⼤云服务商的合计营收积压已达 1.8 万亿美元，同
⽐增⻓约 140% 。部分超⼤规模云计算商已公开披露，预计约 50% 的积压订单将在未来两年内转
化为收⼊。
" 可⻅度从来不会像投资者希望的那样快速出现， "Sheridan 说， " 但企业已经取得了实质性进

展。 "
资本市场：已现裂痕
资本市场的压⼒信号同样不容忽视。
⾼盛全球杠杆融资负责⼈ Miriam Wheeler 指出，⾼收益债市场的 AI 相关交易节奏已⼤幅放缓。
今年夏天，市场⼀度预期秋季将有 600 亿美元的新发⾏量，利差因此⼤幅⾛阔。过去⼀个⽉，⾮
投资级市场⼏乎没有新交易落地。
" 分化将很可能继续成为⾼收益市场的主导主题， "Wheeler 说， " 这与整个更⼴泛市场格局中正在
发⽣的动态相呼应。 " 她预计，顶级开发商和项⽬将继续获得更便利的融资渠道，⽽其他参与者
则⾯临更⼤挑战。
⾼盛⾸席信⽤策略师 Amanda Lynam 的数据则揭⽰了更深层的问题：市场饱和，⽽⾮融资成
本，才是当前最⼤的制约因素。截⾄ 2026 年 11 ⽉初，五⼤超⼤规模云计算商年内已发⾏ 2960 亿
美元债务，超过⾼盛此前 2500 亿美元的全年预测。此外，这五家公司还有 1.7 万亿美元的租赁承
诺（其中 75% 尚未⽣效）和 1.9 万亿美元的其他采购承诺 —— 后者中有 6850 亿美元将于 2027 年底

前到期。
" 评级机构已采取更为谨慎的⽴场， "Lynam 写道。 2026 年 9 ⽉，穆迪指出超⼤规模云计算商的总
体财务承诺⾃ 2023 年以来增⻓了⼋倍； 10 ⽉，标普则表⽰ "AI 资⾦⽣态系统中的容错空间正在收
窄 " 。
如果需求未能兑现
Palihapitiya 对最坏情形也作出了评估，但他并不认为会重演 1999 年式的崩盘。
" 更可能出现的是⼀段理性化时期， " 他说。终端客⼾可能拒绝增加⽀出；债务市场若对利⽤率假
设失去信⼼，资⾦成本将上升 ——" 最弱的信⽤主体可能⾯临接近 20% 的借贷成本， CDS 利差可
能接近 1000 个基点，即便是投资级借款⼈也可能⾯临接近两位数的融资成本。 "
他认为，更可能的结局是资产的重新分配，⽽⾮崩溃。超⼤规模云计算商凭借强健的资产负债
表和核⼼业务，将成为天然的资产买家。

### Page 3

Sheridan 对此表⽰认同，并补充了三个可能触发周期放缓的条件：" 如果需求令⼈失望、资本获
取渠道受阻，或 AI ⽣态系统发现⾃⾝电⼒短缺 —— 当前的幻灭⾕底可能演变为更持久的收缩状
态。 "

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 318: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

## Document 333: CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận - Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận -  Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV   Nguồn: PNJ, KBSV

Doanh thu thuần/ngày Doanh thu bán lẻ/ngày

### Page 2

2

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

## Document 350: Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FOLLOWING THE OIL SHOCK, EL NIÑO THREATENS TO HURT MACROECONOMIC RESILIENCE IN SUB-SAHARAN
AFRICA FURTHER
Lucas Plé
Seven months after the start of the war in the Middle East, which has led to a sharp rise in oil prices, sub-Saharan Africa is holding up well
overall. The deterioration in external accounts and the rise in inflation are more contained than in 2022, during the last oil shock. However,
this resilience is fragile and is eroding as the conflict drags on. Furthermore, an exceptional El Niño event could significantly worsen the

macroeconomic environment in 2027 1.
Economic growth remains robust
For countries with available data, GDP growth held up well in the first half of 2026 2. In mid-October, the IMF revised its regional forecast for
the full year upwards to 4.5% (+0.2 percentage points compared with April), in line with the performance for 2025.
1 El Niño 2026–2027: multiple pressures on prices and supplies
2 1.4% year-on-year (y/y) in South Africa, 4.2% in Nigeria, 5.1% in Kenya, 7% in Angola, 7.3% in Ghana.

Sub-Saharan Africa is highly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 21 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A mitigated shock on terms of trade
High prices for export commodities (gold, copper, platinum) have helped to cushion the impact on terms of trade and the external
accounts of hydrocarbon-importing countries. For the 10 countries in our sample (see heatmap), the depreciation of their currencies
against the US dollar since last February has been moderate overall (2.3% on average).

Inflation has started to rise again, but it is still being contained so far
In the ten countries in our sample, although inflation is rising again, it is lower year-on-year for the first eight months of 2026 than it was
for the same period in 2022. This is due to:
1. Monetary policies that were still restrictive before the conflict started;
2. Fuel subsidies;
3.   Macroeconomic reforms implemented in recent years (a return to orthodox monetary policy in Nigeria, and efforts towards fiscal

consolidation in many countries);
4. Moderate currency depreciation since February.
As a result, few central banks have tightened their monetary policy since February (South Africa, Uganda and Tanzania).
However, this resilience is still fragile…
On the one hand, governments that have chosen to cap fuel price rises are seeing their public finances deteriorate. In Senegal, energy
subsidies will go above 3% of GDP in 2026, which is unsustainable. On the other hand, the region’s external accounts are exposed to

volatile global commodity prices and tighter international financing conditions.
… and the macroeconomic environment could quickly worsen in 2027, due to an exceptional El Niño event
The region is particularly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel. Given agriculture’s high share in its GDP ,
sub-Saharan Africa is more exposed to downside risks to growth than Latin America, Asia or Central Europe (1 st column of the heatmap).

It is also more exposed than other regions of the world to the inflationary effect of rising food prices, given their higher share in the
consumer basket (2 nd column). Finally, even though its dependence on food imports is lower than in Asia and Europe (3 rd column), the
region is highly vulnerable to rising international food commodity prices due to its fragile external accounts. For several countries in our
sample, foreign exchange reserves cover less than four months’ worth of imports of goods and services (Uganda, Tanzania, Ghana and

Zambia), leaving little room for manoeuvre for absorbing a further external shock.
In addition to shocks via the agricultural commodities channel, other localised risks must be considered. In Southern Africa, El Niño is
expected to intensify droughts and threaten electricity generation as a result, particularly in Zambia (where hydropower accounts
for nearly 80% of domestic electricity generation). In East Africa, the increased flooding that El Niño is expected to cause will threaten

infrastructure – particularly logistics infrastructure – which is already less efficient than the global average 3. These two sources of
vulnerability could also increase pressure on growth, prices and the external accounts.
In conclusion, if oil prices remain high and El Niño is exceptionally severe, the region will struggle to absorb this double shock in 2027.
Despite their status as net exporters of hydrocarbons, Nigeria and Angola are also exposed to a deterioration in the macroeconomic

environment in 2027. Only South Africa appears to be better positioned to absorb the shock.
lucas.ple@bnpparibas.com
3 See the World Bank’s Logistics Performance Index.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUSTRALIAN DWELLING
FINANCE BULLETIN
Steep fall
11 November 2026
Key points
• The value of new dwelling finance
approvals excl. refinance dropped
15.5%qtr, the biggest quarterly decline
on records going back to 1991. Annual
growth swung to –18.1%yr. While the

decline was widely expected, it was
larger than the median forecast of -8.5%
and Westpac’s forecast of –10%.
• The quarterly drop was mostly driven
by lower volumes: total loan numbers
down 15%qtr (-21%yr). Smaller average
loan size contributed a further 0.5ppts
to the value decline in the quarter.
• Investors were the primary driver,
volumes in this segment down
over 30%qtr and the value of new
loans off by nearly a third.
• Borrowing by owner occupiers

significant but somewhat milder
declines, both the volume and value
of new loans down 5.3%qtr.
• More weakness looks likely for Q4.

finance:
–15.5%qtr,
–18.1%yr

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The value of new dwelling finance approvals fell steeply
in Q3, a 15%qtr drop to be down 25% year to date. The
quarterly fall was the largest since the series began in
1991 while the fall over the last nine months is on a par
with the 2022 downturn, which was the largest on record.

The outcome was significantly weaker than the consensus
forecast of an 8.5% drop. The downturn reflects the
combined impact of rising interest rates and major housing-
related tax policy changes announced at the May budget
which have hit investor loans for the purchase of existing
dwellings particularly hard.
The detail shows volumes were the main driver, the number
of new loans dropping 15%qtr with the average loan size
accounting for 0.5ppts of the quarterly decline in the total

value of new loans.
The segment split showed a 5.3%qtr fall in new owner
occupier loan approvals and a whopping 30% drop in new
investor loans. Within the latter, the number of investor
loans for the purchase of existing dwellings (which no longer
receive ‘negative gearing’ and capital gains tax concessions)
fell 35.6%qtr and are down by a half over the year to date. In
contrast, the number of investor loans for the construction or
purchase of newly built dwellings (which are still eligible for

concessions) declined 8.7%qtr and 8.5% year-to-date. This
segment now accounts for 27.6% of all investor loans, up from
17% late last year.
Owner-occupier loans reflected the wider influence of interest
rate and market uncertainty. The detail here shows a more
pronounced 8.6%qtr drop in first home buyer numbers, a more
interest rate sensitive segment. High construction costs also
look to be a factor with the number of owner occupier loans for
the construction and purchase of newly built dwellings down

11.9%qtr. Lending for land purchases and renovations also
declined 12.9%qtr in value terms.
Today’s results were weaker than expected with a bigger
pull-back in investor activity than we assessed when the tax
policy changes were announced back in May. Some of this may
be an overshoot with uncertainty about policy changes and
the wider price correction discouraging investors who might
otherwise have considered purchasing even without the tax
benefits. That said, conditions look far from conducive for even

a muted bounce near term. More weak reads look likely for the
final quarter of 2026.

## Document 442: US SECTORS CALL_ Information Technology, Industrials & Utilities

Source PDF: all report pdf/US SECTORS CALL_ Information Technology, Industrials & Utilities.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US SECTORS CALL: Information Technology, Industrials & Utilities
Date:    21 minutes ago
US SECTORS CALL: Information Technology, Industrials & Utilities

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US SECTORS CALL: Information
Technology, Industrials &
Utilities
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 8 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Element5 Digital / Unsplash

### Page 2

Last Thursday’s selloff in the stock market was an AI selloff. Investors
dumped companies that build, wire, and power data centers, following the
Democrats' sweep of Congress amid voter hostility toward new facilities.
Stocks recovered some ground on Friday. October payrolls rose by
165,000, beating expectations of 65,000. Still, the S&P 500 ended the
week down 2.7%, and Information Technology (MW) fell 6.9% (chart).
Energy (OW) was the worst-performing sector this week, down 11.9%, for

a different reason. Oil prices tumbled on hopes of a US-Iran peace deal
following the midterm elections. We are retaining our overweight
recommendation on Energy as a hedge against re-escalation. Consumer
Discretionary (UW) and Communication Services (MW) led the week, up
2.7% and 2.3%.
The market is pricing the AI backlash as a roadblock. We see a speed
bump. Investors have already cut the multiples of the build-out’s suppliers.
Analysts have yet to cut their earnings estimates. That is the next test. We

are not changing any of our sector weights. Here’s more on Information
Technology, Industrials, and Utilities:
(1) Information Technology. The selloff hit the build-out’s hardware
suppliers the hardest. Semiconductor Equipment fell 14.5% this week,
followed by Semiconductors (-10.9%) and Communication Equipment
(-9.4%) (chart).

### Page 3

Semiconductor Equipment is the clearest case of a speed bump priced as
a roadblock. The industry’s stock price index is 27% below its 200-day
moving average (chart). Yet its forward earnings and forward profit margin
are at record highs. Its forward P/E has dropped to 21.2 from about 52.3
in June.
Analysts keep raising their estimates for Semiconductor Equipment
stocks. Collectively, they expect the industry’s earnings to grow 35.4% this
year and 51.5% next year (chart). Their consensus earnings estimate for

2027 has risen steadily all year. Semiconductor Equipment is one of only
two IT industries for which analysts expect faster growth next year than
this year.

### Page 4

Valuations are far from tech-bubble extremes. IT and Communication
Services together account for 47.6% of the S&P 500’s market
capitalization and a nearly identical 47.1% of its forward earnings (chart).
In 2000, when valuations were at tech-bubble extremes, their market-cap
share far exceeded their earnings share.
Tech's forward P/E has fallen to 17.3, and Semiconductors' forward P/E is
down to 12.0. That already discounts some earnings disappointment. A

modest cut in estimates is probably priced in. A deep one is not. That’s
why we remain at a market weight rating.
(2) Industrials. The AI-exposed industries led Industrials lower this week.
Electrical Components & Equipment fell 9.1%, and Construction

### Page 5

Machinery & Heavy Trucks fell 7.8% (chart). Passenger Airlines, Industrial
Conglomerates, and Industrial Machinery all rose.
Electrical Components shows the same disconnect as Semiconductor
Equipment. Its stock price index is 13.2% below its 200-day moving
average. Yet its forward revenues and forward earnings are at record
highs, and its forward profit margin rose to a record 18.2% (chart).
Analysts expect the industry’s earnings to grow 20.5% this year and

19.1% next year. Its forward P/E has fallen to 23.0 from over 30.
The Construction Machinery & Heavy Trucks index is trading at 26%
below its 200-day moving average, even as its forward earnings have
risen to a record high. Elsewhere, Rail Transportation is up 24.4% ytd.
Analysts have trimmed the sector’s 2027 earnings growth estimate to

### Page 6

15.8%, from 19.0% in July. That is still strong growth, and it supports our
overweight rating.
(3) Utilities. The gap between this sector’s best and worst performing
industries is enormous (chart). The Independent Power Producers &
Energy Traders industry stock price index is down 18.2% ytd, including a
7.8% drop this week. Water Utilities is up 15.2% ytd, while the Utilities
sector as a whole is up just 1.0% ytd.
IPPs sell power to data centers, and investors are pricing out that

premium before analysts do. The industry’s forward earnings is up 8.4%
ytd, while its stock price index is down 18.2% (chart). Its forward P/E has
fallen to 10.0, and its forward profit margin is at a record high.

### Page 7

Analysts are starting to follow investors’ lead. They cut their 2026 revenue
growth estimate for IPPs to 15.7% from 21.6% over the summer (chart).
But they still expect IPP earnings to grow 12.0% next year. The low
valuation suggests that investors expect more estimate cuts.
We downgraded Utilities to market weight on October 2 due to rising bond
yields. The data center premium coming out of the IPPs gives us a second
reason. We remain at a market weight rating.

clause.

## Document 517: フィリピン中銀、原油高とペソ安の「二重苦」に直面し、大幅利上げに舵 ～インフレ圧力の高まりを受けて利上げ幅を拡大、金融引_efcf5aff

Source PDF: all report pdf/フィリピン中銀、原油高とペソ安の「二重苦」に直面し、大幅利上げに舵 ～インフレ圧力の高まりを受けて利上げ幅を拡大、金融引_efcf5aff.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/2 
フィリピン中銀、原油高とペソ安の「二重苦」に直面し、大幅利上げに舵 
～インフレ圧力の高まりを受けて利上げ幅を拡大、金融引き締めの長期化も～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ フィリピン中銀は、10 月 22 日の定例会合で政策金利を50bp 引き上げ、5.50％とすることを
決定した。４会合連続の利上げとなり、今回は利上げ幅を拡大するなど、金融引き締めの度
合いを強化した。背景には、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う原油高や、金融市場における通貨ペソ
安の進行を受けてインフレ圧力が強まったことがある。９月のインフレ率は前年比＋7.2％と
中銀の目標を大幅に上回っている。中銀はインフレ率が 2027 年も高水準で推移し、2028 年
に目標中央値の３％に近付くとの見通しを示した。レモロナ総裁は、今回の利上げが為替安
定を目的とするものではないと強調する一方、追加利上げや緊急利上げの可能性を排除しな
い姿勢を示した。先行きもイラン情勢の不透明感やペソ安圧力が続く可能性があり、さらな
る金融引き締めを迫られる展開も予想される。

フィリピン中央銀行は、10 月 22 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会において、政策金利である
翌日物リバースレポ金利を 50bp 引き上げ、5.50％とすることを決定した。同行は、４月の定例会
合で約２年半ぶりの利上げに舵を切り、その後も６月、 ８月の定例会合で追加利上げを実施してお
り、今回で４会合連続の利上げとなる。さらに、今回は利上げ幅を拡大させるなど金融引き締めの
度合いを強めており、政策金利も2025 年６月以来の高い水準となった。 
背景には、イラン情勢の悪化による原
油高が世界的なエネルギーインフレを招
くなか、同国は原油需要の９割以上を輸
入に依存しており、その影響に晒されや
すいことがある。直近９月のインフレ率
は前年比＋7.2％と中銀の目標レンジ （２
～４％）を大きく上回っている（図１） 。
さらに、原油などエネルギー資源価格の
高止まりは輸入額の増加を通じて対外収
支を悪化させており、金融市場では通貨
ペソの対ドル相場が最安値を更新するな

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
ど、輸入物価の押し上げがインフレ圧力を一段と増幅させる懸念も高まっている。このため、中銀
としては物価安定に向けた『より強力な』対応を示す必要があるとの判断から、今回は市場予想を
上回る大幅利上げに舵を切ったと考えられる。 
会合後に公表した声明文では、 先行きの物価動向について 「今後数ヵ月は一段の加速が見込まれ
る」としたうえで、 「インフレ率は2027 年も高水準で推移し、2028 年には目標の中央値 （３％） に
近付く」 との見通しを示した。 さらに、 「コアインフレ率も上昇が予想され、 潜在的なインフレ圧力
の高まりを示唆している」 との見方を示した。 その理由として、 「イラン情勢の再燃による原油価格
の上昇」 、 「交通運賃の上昇によるインフレの上振れリスクが高まっている」ほか、 「エルニーニョ
現象による農業生産や価格へのリスク」を挙げた。一方で、景気動向について「先行きは財政支援
による回復が見込まれる」との見通しを示した。 
今回の決定については 「インフレ圧力の高まりと、 さらなる上振れリスクに対して強力な政策対
応の必要性を認識した」との考えを示した。先行きについて「今回の対応が金融環境を適切に抑制
的なものにする」 、 「副次的な影響を含めてインフレ期待をしっかりと抑える」 との見通しを示した。
また、先行きの政策運営については「物価安定という主要任務に基づいて、インフレ率を３％に戻
すべくあらゆる措置を講じる用意がある」 と過去数会合と同じ文言を載せており、 追加利上げに含
みを持たせた。 
会合後の記者会見に臨んだレモロナ総裁は、 物価見通しについて 「これまでの想定に比べて悪い」
との見方を示した。今回の決定を受けた政策スタンスについて「抑制的であるが、十分に抑制的で
あるかは定かでない」 と追加利上げに含
みを持たせた。 今回の利上げについて
「ペソ相場の安定を目的としたもので
はない」 、 「われわれは多少為替介入を実
施しているが、 相場の乱高下を防ぐこと
を目的としたもの」として、為替安定の
ための利上げではないと付言した。 とは
いえ、 「ペソ安はインフレを招く恐れが
あり、懸念している」と述べるなど、そ
の動向を注視する姿勢をみせた （図２） 。
先行きの政策運営について 「さらなる利
上げの可能性を排除しない」、「利上げ幅については 25bp か 50bp、それ以上かは言えない」としつ
つ、 「緊急利上げの必要性も議論された」 とした。こうした姿勢は、 中銀が切迫した状況に直面して
いることを示唆している。イラン情勢は見通しが立ちにくく、ペソ相場についても下落圧力に晒さ
れる展開が続く可能性があり、その動向には注意が必要である。

### Page 1

市场研究部 （宏观经济报告） 2026年 11月 5日
三季度 GDP价格缩减指数高位小幅回落
三季度 GDP同比增长 4.5%，比二季度回升 0.2个百分点，GDP价格缩
减指数为 101.44，比二季度回落 0.09个百分点，连续 4个季度回升后
小幅回落，仍位于 100之上。其中，第一产业增加值价格缩减指数为
98.39，比二季度提高 1.58个百分点，连续 8个季度低于 100第二产业
增加值价格缩减指数为 103.51，比二季度提高 0.58个百分点，创 2022
年四季度以来 16个季度新高。
三季度消费价格同比涨幅收窄
三季度 CPI同比上涨 0.733%，比二季度回落 0.400个百分点。其中，
食品价格同比下降 1.533%，降幅比二季度收窄 0.100个百分点，非食
品价格同比上涨 1.200%，比二季度回落 0.533个百分点，核心 CPI同
比上涨 0.967%，比二季度回落 0.133个百分点。
三季度 PPI同比涨幅扩大
三季度，PPI同比上涨 4.100%，比二季度提高 0.500个百分点。其中，

生产资料 PPI同比上涨 5.433%，比二季度提高 0.6个百分点；生活资
料 PPI同比下降 0.467%，降幅比二季度收窄 0.433个百分点，
在生产资料中，采掘工业 PPI同比上涨 19.867%，比二季度提高 5.567
个百分点；原材料工业 PPI同比上涨 7.400%，比二季度回落 0.900个
百分点；加工工业 PPI同比上涨 3.200%，比二季度提高 0.933个百分
点。
三季度工业行业产能利用率保持较低水平
三季度，全国规模以上工业产能利用率为 73.1%，比二季度提高 0.1个
百分点，比 2025年三季度下降 1.5个百分点。
其中，采矿业产能利用率为 69.50%，比二季度提高 0.40个百分点；制
造业产能利用率为 73.30%，比二季度回落 0.20个百分点；电力、热力、
燃气及水生产和供应业产能利用率为 72.6%，比二季度提高 2.20个百
分点。
石油和天然气开采业业产能利用率为 91.1%，化学纤维制造业产能利用
率为 84.9%，计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业产能利用率为 80.2%。

三季度，我国国民经济顶住压力延续总体平稳、向新向优的发展态势，展现出
强大的发展韧性和活力。GDP价格缩减指数和居民消费价格指数比二季度小幅回落，
生产者出厂价格同比涨幅比二季度小幅扩大，工业产能利用率仍处于较低水平。
一、三季度 GDP价格缩减指数高位小幅回落
三季度，GDP价格缩减指数仍位于 100之上高位，比二季度小幅回落。

度提高 0.11个百分点。

GDP价格缩减指数为 101.44，比二季度回落 0.09个百分点，连续 4个季度回升后
小幅回落，仍位于 100之上。

上涨行业中，
煤炭开采和洗洗业同比上涨 84.6%；

和 63.0%，石油和天然气开采业 PPI同比上涨 37.5%；

化学纤维制造业 PPI 同比上涨 30.0%，计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业
PPI同比上涨 15.0%。
下跌行业中，
酒、饮料和精制茶制造业 PPI同比下降 16.2%；
医药制造业 PPI同比下降 11.1%；
电力、热力生产和供应业 PPI同比下降 11.4%；
非金属矿物制品业 PPI同比下降 9.6%。

与二季度相比，三季度大类行业 PPI波动较大的是：
比二季度提高幅度较大的行业，
煤炭开采和洗选业 PPI同比上涨 84.6%，比二季度提高 54.6个百分点；
化学纤维制造业 PPI同比上涨 30.0%，比二季度提高 9.3个百分点
计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业 PPI 同比上涨 15.0%，比二季度提高 8.1
个百分点；
燃气生产和供应业 PPI同比上涨 13.5%，比二季度提高 7.8个百分点。

### Page 13

比二季度下滑幅度较大的行业，

3Q26 EPS 37.67 元，分別低於元大/市場預期 1~3 成 
3Q26 台幣營收 QoQ+241%、YoY+297%，主要受到 TRN3 大量出貨，整體
N3/N2 的營收占比從 47%跳增至 80%。惟受到量產營收佔比提高、部份專
案 NRE 遞延認列，使得本季毛利率季減 16.8 個百分點達 18%及 OPM 季減
7.6 個百分點達 13.9%，低於公司先前預期 2 個百分點，業外收益 4.53 億元、
EPS 37.67 元/季增 88%，獲利分別於元大/低於市場預期 1~3 成。 
4Q26 營收、獲利將創新高 
展望 4Q26 TRN3 持續大幅放量及新專案 tape out ，營收、獲利持續創新高，
其中預期毛利率估計與 3Q26 接近，2026 全年營收估計落在 21~22 億美元、
毛利率>20%。元大預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+15%、YoY+536%、毛利率估計
18%、推估 EPS 43.17 元/季增 15%，較前次報告下修 15%，雖然上修營收
預估，但下修毛利率假設基礎。 
TRN3/4 推升 26~28 年營收達新高，第二家 CSP 具想像空間 
展望 2027 年預估營收有機會年增三位數，主要成長來自 TRN3 本身出貨倍
增及公司取得更高的量產配置外，2Q27 新增一家美系 CSP 客戶，估計訂單
規模達 10 億美元，細節不透露。下一代 TRN4 製程採 N2 GAA 的 Chiplet
架構、 2.5D 封裝，公司負責所有 Die 的 physical design 與整合，包括
compute die 與 I/O die ，整體 N2 專案的設計、整合及量產複雜度提高，估
計單價、潛在市場規模顯著超過 N3。中長期 CSP 由傳統晶片統包轉向
COT 分工模式，趨勢對公司有利。最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 118/186 元，
分別下修 15/上修 8%，係因 TRN3 出貨上修及新增 CSP 客戶而上修營收預
估，然毛利率表現低於公司先前指引而下修假設基礎。

元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 EPS 37.67 元，低於預期。展望 4Q26 TRN3 量能放大、新專案
tape out，營收、獲利將創歷史新高，推估 EPS 43.17 元。  
⧫  
展望 2027 年 AWS TRN3 仍為主要成長動能，除了 TRN3 本身出貨倍
增及公司量產配置提高， 2Q27 新增第二家 CSP，將帶動營收有機會
年增三位數，TRN4 接續 27~28 年動能更為強勁。 
⧫  
最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 11 8/186 元，分別下修 15%/上修 8%，雖然
營收展望樂觀，然毛利率低於預期。維持買進評等、惟 27 年目標本益
比降至 25 倍，推得目標價 4600 元，儘管看好 TRN 3 、4 帶動未來
2~3 年成長趨勢不變，然短期評價受系統性風險、籌碼面而有所下修。

圖 3：AI 晶片市場 2025-30 年 CAGR 為 32% 圖 4：預計 2026/2027 年，ASIC 對 CoWoS
